localrule qc_merqury_unphased:
    input: data/ref/hg002_q100_meryl/hg002_q100_k_37.meryl, assembly/output/gfase/TUE_02_02UL/assembly.fasta
    output: assembly/qc/unphased_verkko/TUE_02_02UL/merqury.qv
    log: logs/merqury_unphased_TUE_02_02UL.log
    jobid: 467
    reason: Missing output files: assembly/qc/unphased_verkko/TUE_02_02UL/merqury.qv; Input files updated by another job: assembly/output/gfase/TUE_02_02UL/assembly.fasta
    wildcards: asm=TUE_02_02UL
    threads: 40
    resources: tmpdir=<TBD>


        INPUT_MERYL=$(realpath data/ref/hg002_q100_meryl/hg002_q100_k_37.meryl)
        INPUT_PAT_FA=$(realpath assembly/output/gfase/TUE_02_02UL/assembly.fasta)
        OUTPUT_PREFIX=$(dirname $(realpath assembly/qc/unphased_verkko/TUE_02_02UL/merqury.qv))/merqury
        LOG_FILE=$(realpath logs/merqury_unphased_TUE_02_02UL.log)
        pushd $(dirname $OUTPUT_PREFIX) >$LOG_FILE 2>&1
        export PATH=$PATH:"$CONDA_PREFIX"/share/merqury/eval
        qv.sh             $INPUT_MERYL             $INPUT_PAT_FA             $OUTPUT_PREFIX          >> $LOG_FILE 2>&1
        rm -r *.meryl >> $LOG_FILE 2>&1
        popd >> $LOG_FILE 2>&1
